# Implementation Notes

## Decisions

* The git repository root is `F:\Godot Project Maker` (the GitHub repo). The spec lives in
  `docs/spec/` (M11 T11.08 removed the duplicate `Implementation Plan and Specs/` folder).
* Local development uses Python 3.12 in `.venv` and Godot 4.7.1 (installed locally;
  4.7.2 is the validated version, so ENV-003 warnings are expected locally).
* T8.06 blob layout assumption: the 47-tile "Godot/Tilesetter" 12x4 sheet is read row-major with the 47 normalized 8-neighbor masks ascending (N=1, NE=2, E=4, SE=8, S=16, SW=32, W=64, NW=128; corner bit only with both sides); the 48th cell stays empty. 16-tile corner sets (4x4) use index bits TL=1, TR=2, BR=4, BL=8. Sheets in another order are not recognized (TILE-006). TILE-006 on a failed blob candidate is only reported when the path hints at terrain (blob, wang, autotile, terrain, corner) or the border match reached 50%, so ordinary 4x4 or 12x4 sheets stay silent. Generated A2 atlas assets use the sha256 of their pixels (not of the PNG bytes, which depend on the zlib version). The Tiled adapter output still records wangid; catalog.assemble rewrites it into {set, terrain, peering} in the detected facets (wangid is dropped there).

T4.01-T4.07 done — runtime world scripts (world_object, spawn_point, door, map_root, scene_router autoload with fade/spawn/camera limits; boot goes through SceneRouter), builder libs (scene_util with instance/add/attach_script/UID, texture_util with cached embedded AtlasTextures, map_util, object_entries, object_index), recipes (object_scenes_recipe with the prop/obstacle/building/chest/sign templates and rpggen/object_index.json, interior_recipe -> game/maps/interior_1.tscn, starter_map_recipe rewrite: 40x24 map with the configured layers, seeded ground patches, WALL border, building at tile (8,6) with the Entrance overridden to the interior, 8 decorations, spawn, reserved npc/enemy/chest spots, ROAD path; tileset_recipe: patterns from objects and FULL collision default for WALL sheets), validate.gd VAL-014/VAL-015, smoke_steps_m4 (enter/exit building, decor blocks) with the runner taking a comma separated list — decisions: chest and sign build as solid StaticBody2D objects with their tags kept (no interactable layer until M6); object scenes also cover single-frame sprite assets; the building exit spawn id is `<scene stem>_exit`; instance overrides need set_editable_instance (probed); headless saves write no scene UID header (probed); the interior holds its own Player instance and a gap in its wall at the exit; REAL BUG FOUND by VAL-014 and fixed: character sheets got the whole sheet's bottom edge as origin (95 instead of 23), now the frame-relative median baseline (ANALYZER_VERSION 3, two goldens updated, sample profile regenerated); scene files written by Python helpers must be UTF-8 (a cp1252 byte broke scene_util.gd once)

T5.01 done — core/ledger.py (file_hash with CRLF normalization, Ledger/LedgerEntry, FileState + classify, is_covered/owner_of, scan(staging, target, old)) — decisions: the ledger does not cover GENERATION_REPORT.md or rpggen/generation_report.json (they are rewritten after promotion and a no-op regeneration must change nothing); project.godot, generator_config.json and asset_catalog.json are merge-managed (never a conflict; the catalog is rebuilt from the target's own catalog so user overrides survive); a file that exists in the target but is in no ledger is user-created and is never recorded; user_seed entries keep the hash from creation; a project without a ledger (generated before M5) gets a baseline ledger of what is on disk
T5.02 done — staging.create_staging has new/regenerate modes (copy of the project incl. .godot/, hard links for binary files under assets/packs, copy excludes .rpggen_*, .git, export/, rpggen/logs, rpggen/results), install_* only write changed content (never in place: replace, so hard links stay safe), prune_packs (REGEN-003 for modified files) — deviations: the prune step runs after the catalog phase and keeps the files of MISSING assets, because the TileSet sources kept by REGEN-003 would otherwise point at deleted textures and fail validation (VAL-003); write_project_files keeps the user's project.godot when regenerating
T5.03 done — core/promote.py (plan, final_ledger, execute with backup + tmp/replace + retries + rollback, rotate_backups, promote/promote_new, restore, editor_open) replaces staging.promote — decisions: a first generation still moves the staging folders (no per-file copy); only a run that overwrites or deletes something makes a backup (so no-op runs do not evict useful backups); rpggen/logs and rpggen/results are copied from staging after the writes (they are not ledger-managed); a kept user edit and a conflict keep their old ledger hash so the file stays MODIFIED; the editor check searches the command lines of running Godot processes for the project folder (PowerShell on Windows, ps elsewhere) because no reliable lock file is known — OPEN: verify against a real editor session in the manual gate; new error code REGEN-005 (editor open) added to the spec copies and the generated tables; CLI generate gained --force-while-open and --git, orchestrator run/run_in_memory gained force_while_open and git_message
T5.04 done — tileset_recipe merges into an existing TileSet (never a new one; tile size kept with TILE-004; layers added by name; CONFLICT and MISSING sources untouched with TILE-004 / REGEN-003; remove_source honored; geometry only assigned when it changed; new tiles for new cells; never remove tiles, TILE-005; collision only replaced when the catalog's collision changed since the last build and the polygons are still the ones rpggen applied, else TILE-007) — decisions: the per-tile collision bookkeeping lives in rpggen/tileset_apply_state.json (the card's fallback) instead of TileData metadata, which was not probed; remove_source is added to FacetsOverride and renamed_from to Asset in the catalog schema; a builder fix found on the way: build_state.json's version was rewritten as 1.0 on every rebuild (JSON floats), which made a no-op regeneration change a file; patterns are still rebuilt from scratch and Godot gives them random sub-resource ids, so the .tres bytes differ after every re-save
T5.05 done — core/pack_diff.py (PackDiff with a renamed map in addition to the card's lists), catalog.apply_renames (an asset gone from the pack whose sha256 matches exactly one new asset with the same variant is a rename: id/path/tile set source/objects follow, renamed_from is recorded), ProjectPage 'Update pack…' (pack picker, archive picker, summary dialog, pack list swap, pack ids kept through workers.analyze_packs and the config), AssetGrid 'Changed in update' chip that opens the audit on added/resized/missing assets — decisions: the old inventory comes from the pack files installed in the target; the wizard state gained changed_paths and pack_updates; starter-config asset ids of a renamed asset are not rewritten (the mapping page refills stale entries)
T5.06 done — core/vcs.py (git_available, init_and_commit with the rpggen identity only when none is configured, never pushes, is_clean), gitignore gained rpggen/results/ (so a no-op regeneration leaves git status empty; the card's list had only rpggen/logs/), Options page 'Initialize git repository' / 'Commit regeneration' check box (state.git_commit, None = default: on for a new project, off when regenerating), the orchestrator commits after the report is written — the .gitignore/.gitattributes templates were created in T1.10
T5.07 done — promote.restore (safety backup of what is replaced, files back, ledger last, files created by the undone generation removed when untouched), `rpggen restore --target X [--backup NAME]`, Results page 'Restore previous' (enabled when a backup exists, confirmation, message) — a restore of an older backup than the newest only undoes that generation's files
T5.08 done — tests/integration/test_regeneration.py (the 7 scenarios on the sample pack with real Godot), builder/tools/dump_map_cells.gd, report.py completed (What to review grouped by priority, Packs and licenses with the source name, Summary, TileSets, Scenes, Animations, Regeneration, Validation, Smoke test, Issues with a file column) — deviations: the smoke table has no ms column and the report has no timestamps so an identical project gives an identical report; a regeneration that changes nothing does not rewrite the report files; scenario 5 compares the .tres after normalizing Godot's random sub-resource ids; scenario 1's git check runs only when git is installed; generate page shows a Retry/Cancel dialog when preflight reports REGEN-005
T5.09 gate (M5) — local status on Godot 4.7.1: unit, GUI and integration suites green (see the commit); all 7 regeneration scenarios pass; docs/regeneration.md documents the three invariants (the generated README.md arrives with T6.15). OPEN for the maintainer: (1) manual check — regenerate while the Godot editor is open: the warning dialog must appear (the process-command-line check has only been exercised with a mock), close the editor, regenerate, reopen the project and confirm the painted map is intact; (2) CI on 4.7.2
T6.01-T6.04 done — runtime addon core: events bus, util (autoloads are looked up by node path, not by global name, so every script parses and unit-tests in the template project, which registers no autoloads), RpgRng, RpgStats/Item/Actor/Enemy/Loot/NewGame/Encounter data, RpgInventory (heal at full hp is refused and keeps the item; the registry is injectable), GameState (JSON numbers are restored to ints), RpgPersistent (acts on its parent, falls back to the parent's entity_id), tests/lib/fixtures.gd (installs Events/GameState/SaveManager/RpgSettings under /root; the root is not inside the tree during --script _initialize, so the fixtures call _ready by hand) — RpgLootTable.roll takes a Variant (RandomNumberGenerator or RpgRng); new_game_data.gd and util.gd are extra files
T6.05 done — RpgActor/RpgMovement/RpgStatsComponent/RpgInteraction/RpgPlayerInput/RpgNpc, builder/lib/actor_build.gd, actor_recipes.gd (actor_base, player, npc, characters/<slug>, npc_fallback) replacing player_recipe.gd and player_controller.gd — DECISION: inherited scenes cannot be saved headless (probe_inherited.gd: the result is flat, no instance= root), and plain instancing would put the player group node under a wrapper whose position is the Y-sort key, so all actor scenes are built by one shared function instead (they cannot drift); users create inherited scenes in the editor; an Area2D only finds a StaticBody2D when it is monitorable too (found by the chest smoke step); without an NPC asset the player's sprite is the NPC (ASSET-022, report note)
T6.06 done — dialogue (line/choice/resource, RpgCondition, runner with events, json import), RpgDialogueBox scene, data_recipe seeds hero.tres/new_game.tres/potion.tres/npc_greeting.tres — deviations: confirm advances the box through its own _unhandled_input (the smoke test sends InputEventAction, which does not press buttons), the player's input is re-enabled two physics frames after the box closes so the confirm key cannot re-trigger the interaction; DialogueBox, HUD, notifications, audio and the pause controller are created by boot under SceneRouter
T6.07 done — RpgChest/RpgSign, chest template in object_scenes_recipe (layers world+interactables, Persistent child, chest_fallback tag + ASSET-023 when only an item icon exists), starter map places the NPC (starter_map_npc_001) and the chest (starter_map_chest_001, potion x1, 20 gold) — chest frames: a darkened sprite is used (the open-frame strip variant is supported through the sprite's open_texture meta but nothing sets it yet)
T6.08 done — SceneRouter push_scene/pop_scene (stack limit 4, payload to setup()), reload_from_state, combat/audio slots, map_entered event, current_map/spawn remembered in GameState — SaveManager.load starts the reload in the background and returns true (watch is_busy())
T6.09 done — SaveManager exactly as the card (tmp, verify, backup, rename, migrations, newer version refused); the checksum is taken over a JSON round trip because parsing turns ints into floats
T6.10 done — RpgSettings (volumes, fullscreen, text speed, UI scale, remapping that replaces the project defaults), RpgAudio (not an autoload), audio_bus_recipe (layout generated with the live AudioServer, audio_index.json) — headless DisplayServer calls are guarded
T6.11 done — theme_recipe (nine-slice texture styles or flat styles, licensed fonts only: ASSET-024), ui_recipes (9 scenes), menu scripts, ui_controller (pause on `menu`), boot goes to the title, VAL-011 walk in validate.gd (real ui_down events, every enabled button must be reached; sabotage-tested by hand) — focus neighbors are wired by the recipe for static lists and by the scripts for dynamic ones (inventory grid, settings controls, save slots)
T6.12-T6.14 done — RpgCombatMode contract, RpgStrings + translations_recipe (CSV seeded once; one Translation .tres per CSV locale; DECISION: registered at boot with TranslationServer.add_translation because a .tres in internationalization/locale/translations fails to load at engine start, no CSV import phase), RpgDebugOverlay (F3, debug_toggle only with the debug_tools feature)
T6.15, T6.17 done (Python side, separate commit content) — README.template.md rendered by staging, runtime_version.py with REGEN-006 and --accept-runtime-upgrade, the report shows the runtime version
T6.16 done — smoke_steps_m6.gd composes the M1 and M4 steps (title_new_game first; npc_dialogue and chest_open after the M1 steps; save/mutate_then_load/pause_menu last), saves go to user://smoke_saves and are deleted; unit tests for every runtime class (tests/unit: stats, inventory, game_state, dialogue, save_manager, settings, actors, runtime_services), integration tests/integration/test_m6_sample.py
T7.01 done — battle data under addons/rpg_runtime/archetypes/turn_based/: RpgBattleCommand (+ attack/defend/item/skill/run scripts and a seeded game/data/battle_commands/*.tres per command), RpgBattleActor, RpgBattleMath (pure damage and escape formulas), RpgBattleText/RpgBattleStrings (battle sentences as translation keys with an English fallback) — decisions: the battle keys live in the archetype's own battle_strings.gd (the shared RpgStrings test requires every key to start with UI_) and the translations recipe merges them into strings.tsv for the turn_based archetype; the item command reads the chosen item from RpgBattleActor.chosen_item because execute() has the fixed (user, targets, battle) signature; the damage table test uses hand-computed values with a stub rng plus pinned values for seed 12345
T7.02 done — RpgTurnManager.order (priority, speed, player side, id: never random) and RpgBattleController (state machine, enemy AI, rewards, level-up notification) — decisions: run() is a coroutine whose awaits only suspend when a UI is attached, so with a synchronous auto_choose a whole battle runs inside one call and the unit tests (which the runner does not await) see it finished; auto_choose is called as auto_choose.call(actor, battle) and target_index counts the living candidates of the command; a dead chosen target is replaced by the first living candidate; MAX_ROUNDS (500) guards a runaway battle; enemy items live in the battler's own RpgInventory (filled without events); the controller grants XP through GameState.give_experience for the live player so experience_gained and level_up are emitted
T7.03 done — battle scene (builder/recipes/turn_based/battle_scene_recipe.gd -> generated/scenes/battle/battle.tscn and generated/scenes/combat/combat_mode.tscn) with RpgBattleScene (battlers, hit feedback, hand-over of the result), RpgBattleUi (commands, item list, target arrow, one-entry log, status panels) and RpgTargetCursor — decisions: Background is a CanvasLayer holding a TextureRect (BACKGROUND asset) or two ColorRects; the command buttons, status rows and battlers are created at runtime from the commands and the encounter, the recipe only builds the containers (unique names); the UI answers request_command() as a coroutine (the card talks of awaiting ui.command_chosen: same flow, no missed-signal race); with auto_choose the UI is not asked and the log is not waited for; the scene pops itself only when the router is idle (a battle that ends inside the fade-in would be ignored otherwise); a screenshot probe (scratch script, windowed Godot) was used once to look at the layout at 640x360
T7.04 done — RpgOverworldEnemy, RpgTurnBasedMode, builder/recipes/turn_based/enemy_recipes.gd (definition .tres per ENEMY asset as user_seed, scene per enemy under generated/scenes/enemies/), starter map places starter_map_enemy_001 at enemy_spot — decisions: inherited scenes cannot be saved headless (see T6.05) so the enemy scene is built by ActorBuild like every actor and gets the enemy script, the Contact area and an RpgPersistent child; the property is the inherited `definition` (a subclass cannot redeclare it) cast to RpgEnemyDefinition; contact is polled every physics frame so an encounter refused while the router is busy is retried; wander uses an RpgRng seeded from the entity id so it never draws from GameState.rng (battles stay repeatable); the mode keeps _in_combat until the result is processed, so the enemy touching the returning player cannot start a second battle; DEFEAT policy: HP is at least 1, then the newest save is loaded when one exists, else the player goes to the map's default spawn with full HP; new info code ASSET-025 (no enemy asset: the player's sprite, tinted, stands in); display names drop animation words (slime_idle -> Slime); an existing (user-owned) starter map is not changed, so a project generated before M7 gets no enemy until the user drags one in (README section 11)
T7.05 done — smoke_steps_turn_based.gd (encounter_start, battle_victory, rewards_granted, returned_to_map, enemy_stays_defeated, defeat_policy) appended after the M6 steps through orchestrator.smoke_steps_for(archetype) — decisions: auto_choose has to be in place before the battle starts, so encounter_start sets SceneRouter.combat.auto_choose together with the smoke profile (attack 99) and battle_victory only waits for the result; the defeat step starts the encounter through combat.start_encounter with an enemy copy (attack 99, speed 99, 9999 HP) instead of the debug console, because the console's encounter command cannot change the enemy; it runs the defeat twice: without a save (default spawn, full HP) and with a save (the saved HP comes back)
T7.06 gate (M7) — local status on Godot 4.7.1: the complete sequence (M1 + M4 + M6 + M7 steps) passes on the sample pack, basic16 (fallback enemy), rpgmaker_like and lpc_like; battle math, turn order and controller unit tests pass; a generated project contains only addons/rpg_runtime/archetypes/turn_based, builder/recipes/turn_based and smoke_steps_turn_based.gd (staging.install_templates takes the archetype; the template project holds all of them); runtime 0.2.0 with changelog and README section; a rebuild leaves game/ and generated/ byte-identical. OPEN for the maintainer: the manual check of the gate (play a battle with keyboard and gamepad, use a potion, run, win, see the level-up message) — the keyboard flow, the item list and the target arrow were exercised through scripted key events and screenshots, a gamepad was not; CI on 4.7.2
T8.06 done (Python side) — rpggen/core/terrain/ (tiled_wang, rpgmaker_a2, blob), assemble hooks, tools/debug_a2_sheet.py, audit page Terrain tab (widgets/terrain_tab.py), A2-style block in the sample pack — A2 quarter table implemented as written in the card but NOT verified against a real A2 sheet (human check pending for the gate); A1/A3/A4 report TILE-006; goldens rpgmaker_like and tiled_meta regenerated (new generated assets / peering replaces wangid); sample pack gains tilesets/meadow_a2.png (tagged A2 through the profile).
T8.01 done — addons/rpg_project_tools (plugin.cfg, plugin.gd, dock/) installed by staging.install_templates (new editor_tools parameter, from features.editor_tools) and enabled by project_settings_recipe (editor_plugins/enabled, merged with the user's) — decisions: Godot 4.7 has the new EditorDock API so the dock is an EditorDock added with add_dock; the dock UI is built in code (asset_dock.gd), not by a scene recipe, so there is no scene to keep in sync and no .tscn path the plugin must preload; plugin.gd load()s the dock script instead of preloading it
T8.02 done — dock/index.gd (object_index.json, new rpggen/character_index.json written by actor_recipes, enemy scenes found in generated/scenes/enemies), category filter, search over name/id/tags, Rescan plus automatic rescan when the index files' modified times change — enemies have no catalog id (name from the file name)
T8.03 done — drag (asset_item.gd returns a files drag) and double-click placement; placement.gd plans the fix-ups (Y-sort parent auto/selected, snap in global space, flip, entity id) and applies them in one EditorUndoRedoManager action "Place <name>" — decisions: an entity id is assigned only to persistent entities (an RpgPersistent child), not to props whose entity_id stays empty by design; the next number is 1 + the highest in the map and is written back to id_allocations.entities; a drop is recognised from the selection change after the drag (selected node whose scene_file_path is the dragged scene); NOT VERIFIED in the GUI editor: that the 2D viewport accepts the files drag and the undo of the fix-ups (only the planning is unit-tested)
T8.04 done — tools/content_checks.gd (VAL-008/009/012/016/017/018/019) shared by builder/validate.gd and the validator panel, Fix entity IDs for the scene being edited (ids of other maps count as taken) — decisions: a door with an empty target_map is not VAL-016 (not wired up yet; removing the demo clears the house door); VAL-009 only for persistent entities; ids are fixed only in the edited scene, because re-packing a closed scene outside the editor can lose editable-instance overrides
T8.05 done — dock/asset_inspector.gd + tools/catalog_edit.gd (effective, recompute_status ported; shared fixtures status_cases.json and hash_vectors.json are checked by Python and GDScript), tools/rebuild.gd (save all, run build.gd as a subprocess, rescan), builder flag --update-ledger (refreshes the hashes of the files the run wrote and the two state files; user_seed entries and files the run did not write are left alone), rpggen gui --target — an edited sheet object is written into the object itself (source user), an asset's edits go to overrides; NOT VERIFIED: the subprocess from the GUI editor on Windows (the console exe next to the editor is preferred; the build itself is tested through the same command line)
T8.07 done — animated tiles in tileset_recipe (frames must be a horizontal run starting at the tile; the cells after the first frame get no tile; TILE-008 otherwise or when tiles already exist there)
T8.08 done — dock/demo_tools.gd (game/demo/, demo entities of rpggen/demo_entities.json written by starter_map_recipe, interior_1.tscn when untouched per the ledger, new_game items, ledger demo_removed + deleted_by_user); Ledger.demo_removed (schema, to/from_json, scan, final_ledger) and BuildContext.demo_removed make the demo recipes skip the demo
T9.01-T9.08 done — action-RPG archetype: addons/rpg_runtime/archetypes/arpg/ (arpg_math, damage_info, hitbox, hurtbox, health, state_machine, player_combat, swing_effect, enemy_ai + components detection/chase/wander/enemy_attack/loot_drop, pickup, arpg_mode, ui/hud, arpg_strings/arpg_text), builder/recipes/arpg/ (arpg_build, enemy_recipes, swing_recipe, hud_recipe, mode_recipe, navigation_recipe as a helper), builder/smoke/smoke_steps_arpg.gd, runtime 0.3.0, README section, ARPG_AVAILABLE = True, orchestrator runs the arpg smoke steps after the M6 sequence — the eight smoke steps pass on the sample pack, basic16 (fallback enemy, generic swing), rpgmaker_like and lpc_like (attack animations); sabotage runs (hitbox that never switches off, no invulnerability, line of sight ignored) each fail the step meant to catch them; the turn-based suite is unchanged; tests/gui/test_wizard_flow.py drives the wizard through the ARPG card to a generated project that passes its smoke test.
  Decisions of M9: (1) the hit area is moved `reach` out from the body center toward the facing, with reach = min(frame size x 0.5, tile x 0.75) per axis (the card says frame size x 0.5, which put the area out of reach of adjacent enemies for 64 px frames on 32 px tiles); shape size tile x 0.9 for the player and x 1.1 for enemies. (2) active_frames are stored in the SpriteFrames as the meta `active_frames_<anim>` (sprite_frames_recipe v2); without them the middle third of the animation is live. (3) Shared runtime files gained small hooks instead of archetype checks: RpgActor.move_locked / facing_locked / animation_locked (RpgPlayerInput honors move_locked), RpgAnimationDriver.has_animation / play_exact, RpgInventoryMenu.selected_item / add_extra_button plus a call to `combat.customize_inventory_menu(menu)` when the combat mode has it (the "Set quick item" button exists only in arpg mode), boot.gd `hud_scene` (boot_recipe sets hud_arpg when the file exists). (4) The player's combat parts are added by actor_recipes through the static helper arpg/arpg_build.gd (loaded by path, so the turn-based project, which has no such file, never touches it). (5) Pickups are built in code (RpgPickup.create), not from a pickup.tscn; they are not saved. (6) The HUD bars are ProgressBars with flat styles; a TextureProgressBar from UI assets is not wired (the coin icon is: an ITEM asset named coin or gold). (7) navigation_recipe.gd holds only the nav-mode helper; the TileSet navigation layer is built by tileset_recipe (v5) when features.navigation is on, because the tileset recipe is shared and cannot preload an archetype file. Pathfinding with agents was not exercised (nav_mode defaults to direct). (8) A fifth recipe, mode_recipe, builds combat_mode.tscn (RpgArpgMode): defeat policy (same as the turn-based one, duplicated because the archetypes are separate folders), quick item (the flag arpg_hud.quick_item) and the inventory button. (9) The starter map places two enemies for arpg (enemy_spot and +4 tiles, ids starter_map_enemy_001/002) and lists both in rpggen/demo_entities.json; starter_map_recipe v6. (10) Unit tests cannot run a real raycast (the root is outside the tree in `--script _initialize`), so the wall rule of the line of sight is checked by the smoke step enemy_detects (a StaticBody2D wall between player and enemy, then removed); the unit tests use fake positions. (11) Smoke steps freeze an enemy (`machine.enabled = false`) while the player is the subject, and enemy 2 stays disabled (`input_enabled = false`) until its own step. (12) secondary_attack is bound but has no behavior. (13) A Node created in a member initializer (`var machine := RpgStateMachine.new()`) leaked in validation, which instantiates scenes without entering the tree (VAL-013): the machines are created in _ready.
T9.08 gate (M9) — local status on Godot 4.7.1: unit (GDScript), GUI and integration suites green (see the commit). OPEN for the maintainer: the manual check (combat feels responsive: attack buffering, visible knockback, telegraphed enemy attacks, controller play) was not done by a human; CI on 4.7.2.
T10.01 done — core/diagnostics.py (make_bundle: system.json, config.json, asset_catalog.json, ownership_ledger.json, build_state.json, results/*.json, redacted logs capped to their last 5 MB at a line boundary, assets_manifest.json with {id,path,sha256,w,h,size}, README.txt; no file with an image or audio extension), `rpggen diagnose`, gui/dialogs/diagnostic_export.py behind the crash dialog button (enabled now) and a Results page button, run_gui now logs to local_cache_dir()/logs/rpggen.log so bundles include the app log — decisions: the redaction (home path in every slash style incl. the doubled backslash of JSON strings, then the user name, only for names of 2+ characters) is also applied to config.json, the catalog, the ledger and the results because the config holds the pack source paths; a failed run folder (a moved staging folder) and a project share the same layout, so one function serves both
T10.02 done — packaging/ (rpggen.spec: two Analysis + MERGE into one COLLECT, rpggen.exe windowed and rpggen-cli.exe console, excludes the unused Qt modules, data files godot_templates without tests/.godot, sample_pack, docs, schemas, style.qss; entry_gui.py/entry_cli.py with freeze_support; version_info.txt; installer.iss per-user with Start-menu shortcut, uninstaller, MIT + notices license page; rpggen.ico made by tools/make_icon.py), THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md, tools/package.py (build, LICENSE_AND_NOTICES.txt, portable zip, installer, frozen smoke test, SHA256SUMS.txt), .github/workflows/release.yml — verified locally with PyInstaller 6.22: the frozen rpggen-cli.exe generates the bundled sample pack (exit 0, 178 MB folder), analyzes the 1,000-image pack with the process pool, and rpggen.exe starts; NOT run locally: Inno Setup (not installed here; the .iss is unexecuted) and the release workflow itself; PyInstaller 6 keeps data under _internal/, so the smoke test passes `_internal/sample_pack`; mutagen is GPL-2.0+ and ships in the bundle — THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md says so and a maintainer should confirm the licensing is acceptable before 1.0

## M11 — pre-release fixes, wizard UX and game polish

Godot 4.7.2 was not available while the first M11 steps were written, so entries marked
"unverified in Godot" were written blind. It was added to the project folder afterwards (git-ignored)
and the following was run with it: the GDScript unit tests (273 pass), the whole integration suite
(`RPGGEN_GODOT=... pytest tests/integration`) and the GUI tests that need Godot. That run found and
fixed: the key prompt text (`as_text()` gives "E - Physical"), the heavy-attack smoke step (a heavy
crit killed the test enemy), the death-policy smoke steps (an autosave in slot 0 made the player
reload instead of respawn), audio left playing at exit ("resources still in use": the smoke runner
now stops every audio player and waits 0.3 s before quitting), and the audio test pack (the header
only `.ogg` stubs cannot be loaded by Godot; the two real encoded files are committed and the
generator keeps them). What no one has done is T11.29, the manual pass.

Known limits — every house leads to `interior_1`, which leads back to the first house; slot keys such as `sfx_step_<surface>` are not allowed by the schema; the Windows taskbar progress was skipped; a game-over screenshot is missing from the docs.
CI after the M11 push — three platform differences surfaced, none caused by M11 itself: the Ubuntu job compared synthetic-pack PNG/zip bytes (zlib differs; now Windows-only, the unit test covers the rest), the cancel test took 21 s on POSIX (only the parent was killed; Godot now runs in its own session and the group is killed), and a regeneration test snapshotted `.git/` (background repack; now skipped). A flaky `audio_basic` failure on Windows did not repeat after the failing assertion started listing the real errors; if it returns, suspect the 0.3 s audio drain in `smoke_runner.gd` on a slow runner.


## M12 — review follow-ups

T12.16 done — tests/gui/test_layout_fit.py walks every wizard page (and the welcome view) inside the real window for both archetypes, both themes and the minimum and default sizes (`wizard.MIN_WINDOW`, `wizard.DEFAULT_WINDOW`); it fails on a widget below its effective minimum size (an explicit minimum, else `minimumSizeHint()`), outside scroll areas, and on a button or combo whose text does not fit. `python -m rpggen.dev layout-shots` writes the same pictures to a folder. At `e8bab6d` the test failed on Mapping (every row 47 of 72 px) and on Audit (the splitter needed 913 px and got 806) and on nothing else. Decision: Qt's offscreen platform on Windows has no font database, so every glyph is a box about twice as wide as a letter (a 30-letter sentence measured 348 px, 165 px with Segoe UI); the test and `layout-shots` therefore load a real font (`dev.ensure_real_font`: Segoe UI or Arial on Windows, DejaVu on Linux) and restore the application font afterwards. The test skips when the machine has no font at all.
T12.01 done — Mapping rows and the sound group sit in a frameless scroll area, rows have the vertical policy `Minimum`, and `reveal_missing` scrolls to the first required role without an asset and focuses its Choose… button (Mapping's own `validate` still blocks Next, so the wizard does not call it today). Tests at the window minimum for both archetypes.
T12.14 done — the Audit splitter has the stretch, the done label the vertical policy `Maximum`, the Size label a fixed width, the search field a smaller minimum width and the detail panel's button rows wrap (`FlowLayout`), which brings the page's minimum width under the window minimum. The panel still needs a scroll area before the window shrinks to 880 px (T12.30).
T12.02 done — `pack_ref_from_source(source, pack_id, known_sha256=None)` takes the hash the analysis already computed (`ProjectPage._analysed_hash`), streams an archive otherwise (`archive.sha256_file`), and `folder_sha256` is now the inventory's identity (`path:sha256` of the non-junk files, links skipped), so config and catalog agree; nothing compares `PackRef.source_sha256` today, so no migration. `staging._place(src, dst, src_hash)` compares an existing copy byte for byte against the inventory hash and never reads the source (a text pack file would otherwise be compared with its CRLF normalized).
T12.03 done — `catalog.dominant_tile_size`, `_State.derive_tile_size` (`rpggen generate --pack` only; `--tile-size N` overrides), the project page sets `base_tile_size` from the catalog and a regeneration keeps the stored one; the Options hint reads the draft's size and the resolution combo marks the preset closest to 20 tiles across "(suggested)". Decision: the config schema's `base_tile_size` enum (8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64) became an integer range 8 to 256, because a pack with 20 or 40 px tiles would otherwise fail validation after being detected (02_SCHEMAS.md updated; the two tests that rejected 15 now use 7). `tiles_hint` shows 40, 22.5 and 11.25 as they are and an unending fraction to one decimal (13.3).
T12.04 done — every command but `gui` (and the default, which forwards its arguments to Qt) uses `parse_args` with `allow_abbrev=False`; a first word that is not an option or an option's value is a command, so a mistyped command exits with 2 instead of opening the wizard. The frozen smoke test (`tools/package.py --smoke`) was not run here (it needs a PyInstaller build).
T12.05 done — `promote.editor_open` reads the command lines of the running Godot processes with psutil (a new dependency, recorded in pyproject, THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES and packaging/rpggen.spec) and compares the project paths they name (`--path`, `--path=`, a positional `project.godot`, the folder after `-e`), resolved and case-folded on Windows; no PowerShell, no substring match.
T12.06 done — `archive.Control` (progress, is_cancelled, budget) threads through `open_pack`, `build_inventory`, `analyzer_for` and `assemble`; `workers.ProgressSink` formats the labels and throttles to ten signals a second (the last of a stage always passes); the dialog keeps "Stopping…" up until the worker has stopped (the auto-hide of `QProgressDialog.cancel` is disconnected). Decision: py7zr reports progress from its own thread, so a 7z archive is extracted through a `WriterFactory` that writes the files itself — an exception raised in `write` reaches the caller of `extract` within one block. `adapters.run_adapters` lets `Cancelled` through (it used to be swallowed as ADAPT-001).
T12.07 done — `RunResult.skipped`, `Callbacks.on_skip`, the worker's `skipped` signal, a "–" row with "skipped: <reason>" on Generate (the fix in the tooltip), "Notes (n)" on Results (skipped checks first) and a "Checks not run" section of the report; `generation_report.json` gets a `skipped` key only when something was skipped. Addition: VAL-022 for `validation.export_check: false`, the twin of VAL-021.
T12.08 done (GDScript verified with `godot-test`, 278 tests) — see the runtime CHANGELOG; `DirAccess.rename_absolute` replaces on Windows (probe), the other platforms are proved by a unit test on CI. New scripts got their `.gd.uid` by importing the template project once (the import also wrote uid files for scripts that never had one; those were not kept). Known: `run_tests.gd` does not exit when a test script fails to parse (the hang stops only at the 600 s timeout).
T12.09 done — RAR entries are extracted from a UTF-8 list (`-scsUTF-8 -ssc`, so the file ARCH-005 keeps is the file on disk), links and junctions are never followed (`archive.walk_files`) and are removed after an extraction or skipped in a folder (ARCH-009), 7z symlink entries are not extracted, and `Budget` is shared by an archive and its nested archives (files and bytes). The symlink tests skip where the account may not create links; the junction test runs on Windows.
T12.10 done — `paths.config_dir()`, `data_dir()`, `cache_dir()` (`local_cache_dir()` stays an alias), `core/cache_policy.py` (migration, LRU pruning of extracted archives by a sibling `<sha>.last_used` stamp, a 1 GB cap shared by analysis, inventories and thumbnails, `.part` files after 7 days), Edit > Storage… and the guide section "Where rpggen keeps its files". `APP_FOLDER` had to stay out of `paths.py`'s upper-case string constants (a test compares them with `paths.gd`).
T12.11 done — `app_settings.store()` (one `SettingsStore` per settings file; `get()` from memory, `update` merges with what another window wrote and writes with `jsonio`, `schedule` debounces by 300 ms and flushes at exit); loading no longer touches project folders, the welcome view lists every recent project at once and a worker (2 s per folder, daemon threads) greys out the unreachable ones, with Locate… and Remove; `GeneratePage` reads `phase_timings` once per run.
T12.12 done — `vcs.commit(target, message, files)`: a repository rpggen creates gets `add -A`; in an existing one only the promoted files, `.rpggen-new` copies, the ledger and the reports are staged (`git add -A --pathspec-from-file`) and committed (`git commit --pathspec-from-file`, which keeps other staged and unstaged work out; git 2.25 or newer); `phase_report` adds REGEN-008 before the reports are written (`vcs.foreign_changes`) and REGEN-007 (reason from git, e.g. a failing hook) after a failed commit, rewriting the reports so that they say so. `git.pack_art` (`commit` default, omitted from the JSON; `lfs`; `ignore`) drives `.gitignore`, `.gitattributes` (path-specific LFS lines `assets/packs/**/*.<ext>`) and a README section 9.1; the Options page has the three radio buttons under "Version control". Addition: ENV-013 (a warning) for `lfs` without git-lfs. `staging.write_project_files` now returns its issues.
T12.13 done — `default_fetcher(url, limit, *, timeout=TIMEOUT_S)`; `update_check._fetch` passes its own 10 s and the swap of a module constant is gone (a test greps `rpggen/` for assignments to another module's constants). Two threads in flight at once each see their own timeout.
T12.15 done (the dry run of the workflow on GitHub could not be made here) — `requirements/release.txt` made with pip-compile 7 under `uv tool run` for Python 3.12 (`--generate-hashes --allow-unsafe`, which also pins setuptools; it installs from scratch with `--require-hashes`), `ci.yml` is `workflow_call` and its Windows job installs from the lock and regenerates it to prove it is current, `release.yml` calls it first, installs from the lock (`--no-build-isolation`) and stops on `*(pending)*` in `docs/coverage.md` when the ref is a tag, every third-party `uses:` is a full SHA with its tag as a comment (SHAs resolved from the GitHub API on 2026-10-07), `.github/dependabot.yml` watches the actions weekly, CONTRIBUTING no longer says to copy the table onto itself. The perf job and the Ubuntu job still use `pip install -e .[dev]` (the PySide6 matrix of T12.22 belongs to Ubuntu).
T12.17 done — `analyzer.prewarm` analyzes every image whose `(sha256, ANALYZER_VERSION)` the cache lacks in a process pool (chunks of 4, pool from 32 images, 64-megapixel images in the parent), each worker writes its cache entry and the results seed `analyzer_for(prewarmed=...)`; `assemble` calls it per pack before the adapters and a cancel inside it is `AssemblyCancelled`. A test compares the catalog with the pool, serially and with prewarm turned off for every synthetic pack (identical); a perf test prints the speed-up and asserts 2x only with 4 or more cpus (not run here: the machine was busy with the integration suite).
T12.18 done — folder packs keep `inventories/folder_<sha256(path)[:16]>.json` (size, mtime_ns, sha256, image facts per file): an unchanged file is not read again, a changed or added one is, a deleted one drops out, `build_inventory(use_cache=False)` (`analyze --no-cache`) reads everything. The blind spot (a tool that rewrites a file and keeps size and time) is in the docstring.
T12.19 done — `godot.run_godot(on_line=...)`: the pipes are read a line at a time, the log file is open from the start and written as lines arrive (stdout and stderr interleaved in arrival order; it used to be stdout, then stderr), `godot.LineForwarder` batches `[godot] ` lines (100 ms, 200 lines) into `on_log` and turns `@rpggen step <phase> <i>/<n> <label>` lines (printed by `Log.step` in build.gd, once per recipe, and validate.gd, once per check, 12) into `Callbacks.on_step`; the phases without the protocol (import, smoke) report their last line instead. The Generate page shows "Building tileset (7 of 17)" under the running phase, `LogView` keeps 5,000 lines, takes batches and says once that older lines were dropped. The CLI prints Godot's lines only with `--verbose`. Measured with the real Godot: the import's 237 lines arrive between 0.06 s and 2.17 s of a 2.7 s run. Warning to self: never start two integration runs at once (they share `user://smoke_saves` and the sample's temp folders; the first full run of this session failed that way and passed alone).
T12.20 done — `staging.discard` deletes `assets/packs/` and `.godot/imported/` from the kept copy of a failed run and writes `FAILED_RUN.md` (what went, how to keep everything) unless `keep_full_failed_runs` (settings, default off; a check box in Edit > Storage…) is on; a test that had no cache isolation wrote the setting into the real settings file once, the file was put back and the module now isolates itself.
T12.21 done — `godot_target.SHA512` holds the real SHA-512 of the 4.7.2 zips for windows, linux and macos (read from the release's SHA512-SUMS.txt on 2026-10-07 and compared with the file by a script; a person should still compare them with the release page, as the new checklist item 7 of bump_godot says); `fetch` requires the pinned value, requires the release's list to agree (ENV-011 otherwise) and falls back to the list alone for another version; a stopped download keeps `<name>.part` and `<name>.part.json` and the next attempt resumes with `Range` / `If-Range` (a 206 appends, a 200 or a 416 starts again); network errors are retried three times (2, 5 and 10 s, cancel is polled while waiting), 4xx answers other than 408 and 429 are not. `tools/bump_godot.py` reads the new release's list first and writes the hashes with the version. `tools/fetch_godot.py` gets a macOS platform with T12.63.
T12.22 done (CI only, the matrix could not run here) — `PySide6>=6.7,<7`; the Ubuntu job has the legs `6.7.*` (GUI tests only) and `latest` (everything); `fonts-dejavu-core` is installed so that the layout test has a real font.
T12.23 done — `_execute` catches `Exception` (Ctrl+C and `SystemExit` keep the old path: discard, re-raise): `crash.log` with the traceback in the kept work folder, BUILD-005 naming the phase, `_fail` writes both reports, so the wizard shows Results with "Export diagnostic bundle" and the command line prints the issue; the bundle picks `rpggen/logs/crash.log` up with the other logs.
T12.24 done — `logging_setup`: a `RotatingFileHandler` (2 MB x 5 backups) in `paths.log_dir()` for the command line and the wizard, `[run_id]` in every line (`run_context`, a filter on the handlers; the id is global, so lines of worker threads carry it too), `read_run_lines` for a run's lines with their tracebacks; every generation gets an 8-hex id stored in `generation_report.json` (the determinism test normalizes it, like the timings) and shown on Results ("Run 3f9a1c2e"); a diagnostic bundle holds `logs/rpggen_app.log` with only that run's lines (the last 300 lines when the project has no run id).
T12.25 is further down (the page view-models are done after T12.26 to T12.29).
T12.26 done (the run on the five held-out packs is the maintainer's) — `rpggen analyze --private-corpus DIR --packs ROOT`: an entry (`tools/corpus_fingerprint.py`) is the fingerprint of a pack (path, size, sha256) and its confirmed profile, no pixels; a pack is found when at least 80% of the entry's files are unchanged; the report (`.json` and `.md`) has grid, usage, role and animation accuracy per pack, precision and recall per role, the confusion matrix and the objects found by sheet and region; the CI job `private-corpus` runs on a self-hosted runner when `vars.PRIVATE_CORPUS` is set.
T12.27 done — the perf tests record their timings (`tests/perf/conftest.py` writes `perf.json` in the format of `customSmallerIsBetter`, `RPGGEN_PERF_JSON` moves it); a new case regenerates the big pack without changes (41.7 s here against the 60 s of section 45; the limit stays doubled for CI); the perf job also runs for pull requests labelled `perf` and publishes the trend with `benchmark-action/github-action-benchmark` (pinned SHA of v1.22.2; alert at 130%, a comment, never a failure). Deviation: the 2x speed-up is asserted for the image-analysis stage (2.3 s -> 1.0 s on 20 cpus), not for the whole cold analysis (4.4 s -> 2.7 s, 1.6x), because the whole includes the already parallel inventory and the adapters, and the synthetic images are tiny; both are recorded. The trend job itself has not run here.
T12.28 done — `--json` on `generate`, `analyze`, `restore` and `diagnose` prints one document on stdout (the human lines go to stderr; schema `rpggen/schema/cli.schema.json`, `docs/spec/02_SCHEMAS.md` section 11; `rpggen/cli_support.py` holds `Output`, `exit_code_for`, `issue_json` and `interruptible`). Exit codes: 0, 1 (BUILD/VAL/SMOKE/REGEN/TILE), 2 usage, 3 environment or input, 4 Ctrl+C, 70 (BUILD-005; also an exception outside a generation). Deviations: ASSET and ADAPT count as input (3), because a required role without an asset is the input's fault, not the build's; the first error decides between 1 and 3, BUILD-005 anywhere wins; a missing Godot is 3 (ENV-001), not a usage error. Ctrl+C: a SIGINT handler sets `is_cancelled` for the length of `generate` (and a second Ctrl+C raises as usual); a Godot that dies of the same Ctrl+C before the poll is still a cancel (`_run_phase_godot` checks the flag too). `--dry-run` is `core/dry_run.py`: the Godot-free part of preflight (`orchestrator.check_target`), the analysis in a temporary work folder, the requirement check (a placeholder role is reported as ASSET-011) and the starter-map plan from the catalog alone (`map_preview.building_info_for`); it never writes to the target, creates no staging and starts no Godot. Not verified here: a real Ctrl+C in a console with a running Godot (the tests raise SIGINT in-process).
T12.29 done — `godot_templates/project.godot` sets `untyped_declaration` and the four `unsafe_*` warnings (property access, method access, cast, call argument) to error; `directory_rules={ "res://tests": 0 }` leaves the test scripts out (they duck-type on purpose, about 1,900 hits, and are not shipped). The runtime addons, the builder and the project tools already had no hit: the count was zero when the warnings were first set to warn, so nothing had to be fixed (the first counts were thousands of cascading "not declared" errors from a run without `--import`; the class cache must exist). `tests/unit/test_strict_gdscript.gd` loads every script under `addons/` and `builder/` and fails on one that does not compile, which is how `godot-test` fails on a warning that is an error (a headless run prints no warnings, only compile failures); checked by adding a bad script. Generated projects get their settings from the builder and are unchanged (determinism test green).

T12.42 done — a third theme choice "system" (the default of a first run; a saved "dark" or "light" is kept; View > Theme > Follow the system). `theme.resolve` turns it into dark or light from `QGuiApplication.styleHints().colorScheme()` (unknown counts as dark; `theme.system_scheme` is the one function a test fakes); `theme.apply_theme` connects `colorSchemeChanged` once and `on_system_scheme_changed` re-applies only while the choice is "system". `theme.TOKENS` holds the canvas colours (`canvas_bg`, `canvas_bg_alt`, `grid_line`) for both themes, `theme.token(name)` reads the one in effect and `theme.signals.changed` tells the canvases: `ImageView` (background and grid lines, redrawn) and the animation `FramePreview` use them instead of hard-coded greys; the checkerboard is unchanged. The map preview widget has no dark canvas of its own (the legend is theme-coloured text), so it needed no change.
T12.43 done — `edits.describe_changes(catalog)` lists every asset override that differs from `asset.detected` (kind role / excluded (role NONE) / grid / collision / animations / other), every folder rule and the objects the user added (`source != "auto"`) or rejected (`UNUSED`); `EditSession.revert(change)` clears the override, deletes the rule, removes the added object or restores the rejected one, each as one undo step. `widgets/changes_panel.ChangesPanel` is the "Changes (n)" tab after Terrain (table: kind, what, detected, now, Revert; a filter by kind; the count ignores the filter and follows every edit, undo and redo; double-click goes back to the Assets tab, clears a filter that hides the asset and selects it). Not counted as changes: Confirm (a status, not an override) and tileset or terrain edits (they are not asset overrides).
T12.44 done — the save header has `saved_unix` and `map_name` (the key from `RpgMapRoot.name_key()`: the new `display_name` export, else `MAP_<ID>`); `RpgSaveLoadMenu.slot_text(label, header, bias_minutes)` (static, so a test can fix the time) makes "Slot 2 — Village · Lv 3 · 1:12 played · 12 Oct 14:05" (a map key without a translation falls back to the prettified file name; an old save has no date; month names are `UI_MONTH_n` keys); saving over a used slot calls `_ask_overwrite` (a `ConfirmationDialog` with Yes/No and the focus on No, or the `confirm_overwrite` Callable hook that tests and the smoke run set); `SaveManager.save` keeps a 160x90 `slot_N.png` of the viewport (skipped headless; the Save menu itself is on screen when the player saves, so the picture shows the dimmed menu over the map — not improved here) and `delete` removes it; the menu shows it as the button icon; the recipe and the menu build their slot buttons from `RpgSaveManager.AUTOSAVE_SLOT` and `SLOTS`, `RECIPE_VERSION` is 6. Smoke: the new step `save_overwrite_confirm` (after `save_slot_1`: a no leaves the save, a yes replaces it, an empty slot asks nothing) is in both integration lists. Found by that step: `RpgNotification._expire` took a typed `Node`, so the timer of a toast that had been dropped (more than three at once) logged "Cannot convert argument 1"; it takes a Variant now. The `MAP_` keys are not in `RpgStrings.KEYS` (a test requires its keys to start with `UI_`); users translate them in `game/i18n/strings.tsv`. The thumbnail was not looked at in a real window.

T12.45 done — `RpgGameState` has `equipment` ({actor id String: {slot String: item id String}}), `EQUIP_SLOTS`, static `slot_of(def)` (WEAPON, ARMOR and ACCESSORY map to weapon, armor and accessory), `equipped_in`, `equipped_items`, `equipped_modifiers(actor, replacing)`, `stats_of`, `actor_of(stats)`, `stat_of(actor, stat)`, `effective_stat(stats, stat)`, `equip_preview(actor, slot, item)` ({stat: delta}, only the changed ones), `equip` and `unequip`. Equipping removes one item from the inventory and puts the old piece back; if the old piece has no room the inventory is restored and the call returns false (and `unequip` fails on a full bag). `to_dict` writes `equipment`; `from_dict` takes only well-formed {actor: {known slot: id}} entries, so a save without the key loads with empty slots and `save_version` stays 1. `new_game` clears it. Combat: `RpgBattleActor.stat(name)` (player side wears the equipment, enemies do not) is used by `AttackCommand`, `TurnManager` ordering and `RunCommand`; `RpgHitbox.build_info` and `RpgHurtbox.defense` call `effective_stat`. UI: the new code-only `ui/equip_picker.gd` (`RpgEquipPicker`: one button per candidate item, "Take off", a coloured BBCode preview of the focused choice, cancel closes) is opened from the status menu (a button per slot, shows the worn item and the stats with equipment applied) and from a new Equip button of the inventory menu (added in code through `add_extra_button`, so no scene recipe changed and `RECIPE_VERSION` of ui_recipes is still 6). `data_recipe` (`RECIPE_VERSION` 2): `_gear()` picks the first usable ITEM asset whose id or path contains sword, axe or bow (weapon) and armor, shield or helmet (armor), seeds `game/data/items/<word>.tres` (+2 attack or +2 defense, max stack 1) and puts it in new-game's items; the English names are in `SEED_STRINGS`. A project whose packs have no such ITEM asset has no starter gear. Tests: `test_equipment.gd` (sums, swaps, refusals, full bag, preview, save, combat reads, picker), `test_data_recipe_gear.gd`; smoke step `equip_raises_attack` (after `save_overwrite_confirm`: a test sword equipped through the status menu raises attack by 3 and the menu label shows it; removed afterwards) is in both integration lists (basic16 slice `[:19]`). `godot-test`: 301 passed; `test_m6_sample.py` and `test_generate_basic16.py` pass (12). Not verified: the starter gear on a project with real sword/armor art (no fixture pack has such an asset; the recipe is covered by unit tests with stubbed catalogs), the picker with a real gamepad, and the inventory menu's Equip button (only the status menu path is in the smoke). Left out: `max_hp` modifiers do not move the HP bar (it reads the base stat); the action RPG's `move_speed` is not routed through the modifiers.

T12.45 follow-up — the two things left out of T12.45. `max_hp`: `RpgUtil.max_hp(stats)` (base `max_hp` plus the owner's equipment modifiers; the base value for enemies and unknown stats) replaces direct `max_hp` reads in `RpgStatsComponent` (damage event, `heal` cap), `RpgHealth` (`max_hp()`, `changed`, `revive`), both HUDs (`RpgBattleActor.max_hp()` for the battle bars), the status menu, `RpgInventory.use`, `RpgDefeatPolicy`, the debug overlay and the dialogue `heal_full` event; `GameState.give_experience` refills to the equipped cap after a level-up. Rule for the hp: equipping a max_hp item does not heal, taking it off clamps `hp` to the smaller cap, never below 1 for someone alive (`_set_worn`). Enemy code (`battle_controller` lines that read `actor.stats.max_hp` for enemies) is unchanged on purpose. `move_speed`: nothing read `RpgStats.move_speed` at all — the player's walking speed was only the `RpgMovement.speed` export (80) — so the stat is now a ratio: `GameState.move_speed_factor(stats)` = effective / base `move_speed` (1.0 when nothing is worn), and `RpgMovement.walk_speed()` multiplies `speed` by it for the player; the dodge speed is multiplied too (not special-cased). Found while doing this: `GameState` emitted `equipment_changed` but `RpgEvents` had no such signal, so `RpgUtil.emit` dropped it silently; the signal exists now and the action-RPG HUD refreshes on it. Tests: 5 new in `test_equipment.gd` (cap, no heal, clamp on removal, level-up refill, walk speed, the event); `godot-test` 306 passed. Not verified: the HP bars with a real max_hp item in a running game, and the walk speed in a running game (unit tests only).

T12.46 done — `data/shop_definition.gd` (`RpgShopDefinition`: `id`, `title` key, `stock` [{item_id, price}] with price < 0 meaning the item's value, `sell_ratio` 0.5; `buy_price`, `sell_price` (value * ratio rounded down, at least 1; 0 for key and quest items and for value 0), `offered(items)`, `buy(game, id, qty)` and `sell(game, id, qty)` returning `""` or `not_for_sale` / `not_enough_gold` / `no_room` / `not_held` / `worthless`; the shops folder constant lives in `RpgGameState.SHOPS_DIR` because a test forbids `res://game/` paths in other runtime scripts). Buying is all-or-nothing through the new `RpgInventory.room_for(id)` (free stack room plus free slots times the stack limit). The event `open_shop:<id>` (malformed without an id) emits the new `Events.shop_requested`; `RpgUiController` remembers it, and opens the shop once `dialogue_finished` has fired and three physics frames have passed (so the dialogue box returns the player's controls before the pause), or at once when no dialogue is open. `_open_menu(name, args)` is now shared by `open_pause` and the new `open_shop` (it sets `args` before `add_child`; the shop uses the same `_menu` slot, so the pause menu cannot open on top of it). `ui/shop_menu.gd` (`RpgShopMenu`): scene `shop_menu.tscn` from `ui_recipes` (`RECIPE_VERSION` 7: Heading, Gold, BuyTab / SellTab toggle buttons, a scroll list `%List`, Description, Back), one button per item ("Potion — 10 G", selling "Potion x3 — 5 G"), pressing buys or sells one and keeps the focus on the same row, refusals show a toast, Back / cancel unpauses; one vertical focus ring (tabs, rows, Back) with left / right switching the tab; with no `shop` set it still opens (the project validator opens it bare), and it is in `validate.gd`'s MENU_SCENES, so the VAL-011 focus walk covers it (the m6 test expects 8 walked menus now). `data_recipe` (`RECIPE_VERSION` 3): seeds `game/data/shops/village_shop.tres` (potion plus the starter gear, at their values) unless the demo was removed, a dialogue line `shop` with the event `open_shop:village_shop` and a choice "Show me your wares" that appears once `flag:starter_npc.talked` is set (the villager then also says "Safe travels!" before the shop opens, because the lines are sequential); the choice helper takes an optional third entry, the condition. Projects generated before this have no shop file and no scene until they are regenerated; the dialogue file is a user seed and is not touched. Tests: `test_shops.gd` (9: prices, buy, refusals, bag and stack limit, sell, worthless, the event, a missing file, menu focus chain on both tabs, menu buy and sell, bare menu, the seeded shop and offer); smoke step `shop_buy_sell` (the village shop through the event bus: it opens paused, buying a potion costs 10, no gold buys nothing, selling pays 5, closing unpauses) in both integration lists (basic16 slice `[:20]`). `godot-test`: 318 passed; `test_m6_sample.py` and `test_generate_basic16.py` pass. Not verified: the shop opened from the real villager conversation in a window (the smoke fires the event directly), the look of the menu, and the gamepad by hand. Not done: quantity selection (one per press), a content check that an `open_shop:` id has a shop file (`VAL-019` does that for items only).

T12.48 done — `data/quest_definition.gd` (`RpgQuestDefinition`: `id`, `title`, `stages` [{id, description, complete_when}], `rewards`; `stage_ids`, `stage_index`, `stage`, `first_stage`). State is the existing `GameState.quests` dictionary: the value is the current stage id while active, `"done"` after (`QUEST_DONE`), so the save format is unchanged and `set_quest:` / `quest:<id>=<state>` keep working; stage ids must not be "done". `GameState` loads `game/data/quests/*.tres` into `quest_defs` (`QUESTS_DIR` is there because of the no-`res://game/`-in-other-scripts test) and has `quest_state`, `quest_is_active`, `quest_is_done`, `quest_start` (first stage, or "active" without a definition), `quest_set_stage` (only for an active quest and a stage of its definition), `quest_complete` (sets done and gives gold, experience through `give_experience` and items once; a second call does nothing), `refresh_quests` and `journal_ids(finished)`. `refresh_quests` walks the active quests and advances past every stage whose `complete_when` holds (a loop with a guard of 64 and a re-entrancy flag, because the rewards change gold and items); it is connected to the Events `entity_state_changed`, `item_added`, `item_removed`, `gold_changed`, `level_up`, `dialogue_finished` and `game_loaded` in `GameState._ready` and also runs after a start or a stage change, so a stage whose condition already holds is skipped at once. Every start, stage change and completion shows a toast (`UI_QUEST_NEW`, `UI_QUEST_UPDATED`, `UI_QUEST_DONE`) and emits the new `Events.quest_changed(id, state)`. Dialogue: events `quest_start:<id>`, `quest_stage:<id>:<stage>` and `quest_complete:<id>` (malformed without ids); conditions `quest_active:`, `quest_done:` and `quest_stage:<id>:<stage>`. UI: `ui/journal_menu.gd` (`RpgJournalMenu`) and scene `journal_menu.tscn` (Active / Completed tabs, one button per quest, the stages of the focused quest as "[x] done" and "> current" lines, Back; one focus ring), in `validate.gd`'s MENU_SCENES (the m6 test now expects 9 walked menus); the pause menu recipe has a Journal button (`ui_recipes` `RECIPE_VERSION` 8) and `pause_menu.gd` wires it with `get_node_or_null`, so a project built earlier still opens. `data_recipe` (`RECIPE_VERSION` 4) seeds `game/data/quests/errand.tres` (rewards 25 gold and 5 experience) and changes the villager's dialogue: the first line starts the quest, a choice "I opened the chest." (shown at stage `return`) leads to a line that sets `starter_npc.reported`, which finishes the last stage. The stages are talk (`flag:starter_npc.talked`), chest (`flag:starter_map_chest_001.opened`) and return; the chest stage is left out when the starter map has no chest (`_has_chest`: the `starter_map` options allow one and the packs have a chest entry) — the basic16 smoke found that without this the quest could never finish. Tests: `test_quests.gd` (11: conditions, events, refusals, automatic stages, skipping ahead, rewards once, toasts, save round trip, journal tabs and focus chain, the demo definition); smoke step `quest_journal` (after `shop_buy_sell`: the demo quest is at `return` after the real talk and chest, telling the villager finishes it and pays 25 gold, the Completed tab lists it, the pause menu has a Journal button; state is restored) in both integration lists (basic16 slice `[:21]`). `godot-test` 329; `test_m6_sample.py` and `test_generate_basic16.py` pass (12). Not verified: the villager conversation with the "I opened the chest." choice in a window (the smoke sets the flag through the event), the look of the journal, and the toasts on screen. Not done: quest markers on the map (since done: T12.64 marks a stage's optional `target`), an `open_shop`-style content check that `quest_*` ids exist, and a quest that gives items shown in its description.

T12.47 done — `RpgGameState` has `MAX_PARTY` (4, the player counted, so `party` holds at most 3), `ACTORS_DIR`, `actor_defs` (id -> `RpgActorDefinition`, filled on demand from `game/data/actors/<id>.tres`, `hero.tres` for the player; tests put definitions in directly), `actor_definition`, `actor_name`, `party_ids`, `in_party`, `join_party` (refuses a duplicate, the player, a full party and a missing definition; copies `base_stats`, signals `party_changed` and toasts `UI_PARTY_JOINED`), `leave_party` (unequips every slot into the inventory first; what does not fit stays in `equipment` under the actor and comes back if they rejoin), `revive_party` (everyone at least 1 HP), `heal_party_full`, `give_experience_to(actor, amount)`; `give_experience(amount)` now grants the whole party (so quest rewards are shared too) and returns the player's levels. The save already had `party`; `from_dict` now skips entries without an id and cuts at `MAX_PARTY`. Dialogue: events `join_party:<id>` / `leave_party:<id>` (malformed without an id), condition `party:<id>`, and `heal_full` heals the party. Battle: `battle_controller._build_party` had been written for a party array; it now takes member names, sprites and portraits from the definitions, `_grant_xp` goes through `give_experience_to` for the player side (so `experience_gained` / `level_up` are emitted per member), and `_end` revives fallen members at 1 HP after a victory or an escape (not after a defeat: `RpgDefeatPolicy.apply` revives or heals the whole party, as it did for the player). A KO'd member gets no experience (the loop was already over the living). `RpgBattleUi` marks whose turn it is with "> Name" in the party panel; target selection and the item list already worked on `candidates()`, which covers the whole living party for ally commands. The status menu (`RpgStatusMenu`) pages with left / right (`_input`, off while the picker is open) or its first button "Member" (only with more than one character, so a one-character game looks as before) and opens the picker for the member on show. `data_recipe` (`RECIPE_VERSION` 5), turn-based demos only: seeds `game/data/actors/companion.tres` ("Mira", hero art, weaker stats) and a villager choice "Would you like to travel with me?" / "You can stay here now." (conditions `not party:companion` / `party:companion`); the join and leave lines sit before `bye`, which has to stay last because a line without `next` falls through to the following one. Tests: `test_party.gd` (18: join and leave, refusals, the events and the condition, gear returns, per-member equipment, battle sides, speed order with two members, targets, shared and fallen-member experience, defeat, quest-reward experience, save round trip, a save without a party, menu paging and picker, the demo dialogue); smoke step `party_members` (after `quest_journal`: the companion, or a stand-in on the action RPG, joins through the event, the menu pages to them, the save data lists them, leaving works) in both integration lists (basic16 slice `[:22]`). `godot-test` 349; follow-up: the inventory menu asks on whom to use an item once there is a party (`ui/member_picker.gd`, `RpgInventoryMenu.use_on`), and the battle status panel uses 9 px text from three battlers so four rows fit (not checked in a window). 347 before it; `test_m6_sample.py` and `test_generate_basic16.py` pass (12). Not verified: a real battle with a companion in a window (units only), the look of four party rows in the 4:3 battle layout, the pager with a gamepad by hand, and the villager conversation. Not done: a content check that `join_party:` ids have an actor file, characters who join at their own level, followers in the action RPG (out of scope for 1.0).

T12.49 done — Tiled and LDtk maps. `adapters/maps.py` (transform bits 1/2/4, `rle`/`unrle`, `marker_kind`, `MAX_CELLS`) is shared by the two readers; they return raw maps in `AdapterResult.maps` (tile layers as `[x, y, image, col, row, alt]`, IntGrid as the full value list, objects only for spawn / door / sign) so no tile set is needed while reading. `tiled.py` decodes CSV, base64, zlib and gzip (the decompressed size is capped at 64 MB), flattens infinite maps to the bounds of their tiles, resolves external `.tsx`/`.tsj` tile sets relative to the map and reports zstd, damaged data, non-orthogonal maps and a missing tile sheet as TILE-009 (the other layers stay). `ldtk.py` reads every level (also `.ldtkl` files), reverses the layer order (LDtk lists the top layer first), turns an IntGrid layer with auto-rule tiles into an IntGrid layer plus a `<name>_tiles` layer, takes the column and row of a tile from its `src` position minus padding and spacing, and calls an IntGrid value solid when it is named wall / solid / collision (or every value of a layer with such a name when no value is named). `catalog.resolve_maps` runs after the tile sets exist: tiles become `[count, source_id, col, row, transform]` runs per tile set (a layer with several tile sets is split as `Layer (tileset)`), a layer none of whose sheets is in a tile set gets TILE-009, and the `solid` flags of the previous catalog win. `Catalog.maps` is optional (`to_json` leaves the key out when empty, so the existing goldens did not move); `generator_config.imported_maps` is `{include, starter}` and is likewise omitted when empty. Godot: `MapBuild.paint_runs`, `solid_rects` (row runs merged with an equal run above), `add_solid_body`, `transform_alt`, `free_cell_near_centre`; `imported_maps_recipe.gd` builds the selected maps (`build_map` is also what the starter map recipe calls when `imported_maps.starter` is set, so the layout planner and the demo entities are skipped; recipe version 8, and `demo_entities.json` lists none, so there is no east exit and no field). Flips use the transform flags of the cell's alternative tile instead of extra alternative tiles in the TileSet: the TileSet is a generated file shared by every map, and Godot applies the flags to any tile. A door whose `target_map` names no built map becomes a plain marker. `validate.gd` round-trips every scene under `game/maps/imported`. The smoke steps that play the demo's villager / enemy pass with an INFO line when the starter map was imported (`smoke/imported_starter.gd`; the quest step also when the villager or chest is missing). Known limits: one tile per cell (LDtk stacked tiles keep the last), no hex, isometric or staggered maps, Tiled tile objects and image layers are ignored. The solid IntGrid values are chosen in the Options page (`SolidValuesDialog`, `options.intgrid_rows` / `set_solid` edit the in-memory catalog, and the choice is carried by `resolve_maps`), not in the audit page.

T12.54 done (amended, see the card) — `events/sequence.gd` (`RpgSequence`: `id`, `skippable`, `commands` [{cmd, ...}]; `REQUIRED` argument table; `problems()` / static `problems_of` and `label_in`, static so the editor's validator can call them on a placeholder resource) and `events/sequence_runner.gd` (`RpgSequenceRunner`, created by boot under SceneRouter as `SequenceRunner`; `instance()` and `running()` find it by path, not by group, so the unit tests, whose root is outside the tree, see it too). The runner advances in `tick(delta)` from `_physics_process` (PAUSABLE, so a shop or menu opened by a sequence holds it): the background tasks first, then the current task, then every command that takes no time; the first task advanced in a frame gets the frame's time. Timed steps end within `EPSILON` (1e-6 s) of their length, because 30 x 1/60 s summed came out a hair short of 0.5 and cost a frame. More than `MAX_INSTANT` (1000) commands in one frame stops the sequence (a loop without a wait). Characters are moved by setting `position` (walls do not stop them, the result does not depend on physics) with `RpgActor.scripted_walk` playing the walk animation; `animate` uses the new `RpgActor.hold_animation` / `release_animation`. `show_dialogue` opens the box with no actor, so the box never touches the player's input; a skip walks the dialogue headless (`RpgDialogueRunner.fast_forward`: events run, the first visible choice is taken) or fast-forwards the open box (`RpgDialogueBox.fast_forward`). `start_battle` waits for `RpgCombatMode.encounter_finished`; a defeat clears the sequence (the defeat policy takes over); the action RPG's mode does not start battles, so the command is skipped with a warning there. `change_map` completes the background tasks first and forgets the camera state (the new map has a new camera). At the end the background tasks finish, held animations are released, the camera offset and lock are reset when the sequence used them, and a sequence that left the screen black is faded back. `RpgSceneRouter.fade_alpha` / `set_fade_alpha` are new. `RpgTriggerArea` (world/trigger_area.gd): walk-in (mask 2) or interact (layer 5), `once` through the flag `<entity_id>.triggered` (set when it fires), `condition`, and `sequence` (kept in the map) beside `sequence_id`. The dialogue event `run_sequence:<id>` emits the new `Events.sequence_requested`; the runner starts it three physics frames after the dialogue closes (as the shop does), or queues it behind a running sequence. Variables: `GameState.variables` with `get_var` / `set_var` / `add_var`, the optional save key `variables`, the events `set_var` / `add_var`, the condition `var:` and `Events.variable_changed` (quests refresh on it). Files outside the card's list, each a one-line gate on `RpgSequenceRunner.running()`: `player_input.gd` (no movement or interaction), `interaction.gd` (no prompt), `movement.gd` (desired direction ignored, so a chasing enemy does not slide on), `ui_controller.gd` (no pause menu), `overworld_enemy.gd` (no wander, no contact battles), `arpg/state_machine.gd` (every action-RPG fighter holds) and `arpg/hurtbox.gd` (no damage); also `boot.gd`, `actor.gd`, `scene_router.gd`, `dialogue_box.gd`, `events.gd`, `game_state.gd` and `condition.gd`. Deviation: the card names `dock/asset_inspector.gd` for the editor part, but that panel edits catalog entries; the tool is in the dock's Project tab instead ("Add trigger area (walk in)" / "(interact)", `placement.gd` `plan_trigger` / `build_trigger` / `make_sequence_file` / `add_trigger`: under the map's Triggers node, snapped, id `<map>_trigger_<n>`, and an empty sequence file of that name). New code VAL-023 (content_checks: `sequence_problems` adds called sequences, dialogues, enemies and maps that do not exist; triggers without a sequence, with a missing one, or `once` without an entity id). Tests: `test_sequences.gd` (28: every command, labels / if / else, call and end, the loop guard, background moves and wait_for, determinism, the queue, skipping, autosave held, the dialogue event, triggers, variables, the dialogue fast-forward), 2 in `test_content_checks.gd`, 1 in `test_placement.gd`; `godot-test` 388. Smoke step `sequence_plays` (after `party_members`, in both integration lists, basic16 slice `[:23]`): a cutscene walks the villager out and back, fades, shows a line, gives a potion, pans and shakes; the player does not move on `move_right`, the pause menu does not open and autosave is refused while it runs; a walk-in trigger fires once in two visits; cancel skips a skippable one; everything is put back. `test_m6_sample.py` passes (9). Not verified: the trigger tool in the GUI editor (its undo and the file it writes; the plan and the file are unit-tested), the camera lock in a window, and a sequence's `start_battle` in a real game (unit-tested with a fake combat mode).

T12.53 done — `data/encounter_table.gd` (`RpgEncounterTable`: `groups` [{enemies: [ids], weight}], `can_escape`; `pick(rng)` draws one number, weights below 1 count as 0; `encounter_for(index)` builds an `RpgEncounterData` with no entity id, so a victory defeats nothing on the map; static `problems_of` for the validator), `world/encounter_zone.gd` (`RpgEncounterZone`, an Area2D that takes part in no physics: `contains()` tests its CollisionPolygon2D / rectangle / circle children with geometry, or the painted cells of `cells_layer`, else of a TileMapLayer named `Encounters` in the map when the zone has no shape of its own (that layer is hidden at `_ready`); `steps_per_encounter` (default 24; a step brings a battle when `rng.randf() * steps_per_encounter < 1`), `disabled_flag` `<entity>.<key>`) and `world/encounter_steps.gd` (`RpgEncounterSteps`, not in the card's list: tiles walked from the player's position each physics frame, a jump of more than 2 tiles is a door or a load and is not counted, `GRACE_STEPS` 6, decremented by any step, set after every battle). `RpgTurnBasedMode.count_steps()` runs in `_physics_process` and resets the counter on `map_entered`, during a battle, a map change or a sequence; `start_random_encounter(zone)`. `RpgUtil.world_position(node)` (global position, or the ancestors' transforms for a node outside the tree) is used by the counter and the sequence runner, so the unit tests log no tree errors. Dock: "Add encounter zone (over the selection)" in the Project tab (`placement.gd` `plan_zone` / `make_table_file` / `build_zone` / `add_zone`): a selected TileMapLayer becomes `cells_layer`, other selected 2D nodes are covered (half a tile of margin, at least 3x3 tiles), nothing selected gives 6x6 tiles at the view centre; the table `game/data/encounters/<map>_zone_<n>.tres` lists every enemy definition as a group of one; refused with a message in a project without the turn-based archetype. Demo: `second_map_recipe` (`RECIPE_VERSION` 2) adds `Triggers/EncounterZone` over the east half of new turn-based fields (x from 12 tiles, inside the walls; 20 steps per encounter) and seeds `game/data/encounters/field_1.tres` (the field's enemies alone, weights 3 and 2, and the first one twice, weight 1); "Remove demo content" deletes the table with an untouched field. VAL-024 (content_checks `_check_zones`: no table, `EncounterTable.problems_of`, no shape and no cells layer). Tests: `test_encounters.gd` (9: weighted and seeded draws, the encounter data and problems, polygon / rectangle / circle, the Encounters layer, the flag, whole steps and teleports, the first battle at the same step for seed 12345, grace steps, the mode with a fake `start_encounter`), 1 in `test_content_checks.gd`, 1 in `test_placement.gd`; `godot-test` 399. Smoke step `random_encounter` (turn-based, after `enemy_stays_defeated`, in `M7_STEPS`): the demo field's zone has a usable table; a 12x12-tile zone with 1 step per encounter around the player starts a battle within one tile, the smoke profile wins it, 6 grace steps follow, and the zone's flag switches it off. Found by the run: the step's cleanup moved the player home after clearing the zone's flag but while the zone was only queued for deletion, so the hop started a stray battle whose VICTORY reached the next step's defeat check; the zone now leaves the tree first and `zone_at` skips zones queued for deletion. `test_m7_sample.py` passes (10). Not verified: the dock tool in the GUI editor (its plan and the table file are unit-tested), random battles on the demo field in a window.
